**我建议的改法**（正是你说的 subagent 化，附带把主上下文的成本拿掉）：
- `compaction_work`（重载荷）→ `compaction_action`（轻指令）：只给 `task_id` + 阈值 + 三步配方，让**异步 general-purpose 子代理**自己调 `compact_task_memories(mode="prepare")` 取条目、写摘要、`submit`。好处是压缩要省的字节不再先经过主 Agent 上下文，且主 Agent 不被阻塞；副作用是摘要质量由子代理负责，它只有 prepare 视野（无本会话上下文）——这点靠 `instruction` 里已有的"覆盖既有摘要/保留结论性信息"约束兜住。

- `task_manager.py`：新增 `_compaction_action()`；`get_task_context` 在 red 档不再内联 `compaction_work`（25 条待压条目正文），改携带轻指令 `compaction_action`；`add_task_memory` 的两处 hint 从劝喻式改成祈使式（"consider compacting" → 写侧只报数 / red 档派发子代理）。
- `registry.py`：`get_task_context` 与 `compact_task_memories` 描述同步新契约。
- `prompts.py`：task-workflow 会话开始新增**第 6 步「记忆压缩（异步 subagent，无确认闸门）」**——原来 prompt 里根本没有这一步，这是 Agent 转而问用户最大缺口；存储约束段同步。

- `tests/test_task_memory_search.py`：两个驱动测试重写，新增断言「条目正文不得泄漏进主上下文」。

**验证结果**
- 全量 `1231 passed / 2 skipped`（ruff check + format 干净）。
- 进程内冒烟（隔离仓库 `.scratch/compaction-smoke`，造 45 条）：`compaction_due=true / level=red`，响应里**不含**任何待压条目正文，`prepare` 仍能取到 25 条 + instruction。

assistant: ## 根因（已实证）

`user_id()`（`codewiki/src/config.py:232`）的解析链是：`CODEWIKI_USER` env > 磁盘缓存 > `git user.email` > `git user.name` > `os.getlogin()` > `local`。**链条上每一环都以进程环境为输入**，而两条通道的环境不同：

| 环境组合（同一条解析链，逐子进程实测） | 缓存 | git email | 结果 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 登录 shell | 命中 | 命中 | `iamwangbao-163-com` |
| 只改写 HOME/USERPROFILE | 仍命中 | 空 | `iamwangbao-163-com`（不漂移） |
| 只把 git 从 PATH 去掉 | 命中 | 空 | `iamwangbao-163-com`（不漂移） |
| **APPDATA 缺失 + HOME 改写** | 空 | 空 | **`Administrator`** ← 精确复现 |

关键：缓存路径是 `APPDATA or expanduser("~")`（config.py:206），git 读 `~/.gitconfig` 也依赖 HOME。IDE 启动的 MCP server 是**服务态进程**——我这次误传漏了 `repo_path`，笔记直接落到它的 cwd `C:\Users\Administrator\AppData\Local\Programs\Qoder CN`，就是这一点的现场证据。HOME/APPDATA 双双不可用时，git 拿不到 email、缓存读不到，退到 `os.getlogin()` = `Administrator`。

顺带一个口径 bug：`_git_config_value` 的 docstring 写「Global git config」，实现却是 cwd 相关解析（config.py:146-167）。

**危害不止渲染错位**：压缩域是「只看自己的分片」，所以 MCP 侧 `prepare` 对 45 条磁盘条目返回 0 条 no-op——同一人的检索、压缩、near-duplicate 拦截（比对范围限自己分片）全部只覆盖一半。`.meta/telemetry/` 下已沉积 6 个身份别名（Administrator / iamwangbao / local / mambo-wang / wandering-bug / wangbao）。

## 改法（四层，可分开做）

- **A. 零代码止血**：在 `~/.qoder-cn/settings.json` 的 codewiki server `env` 块加 `CODEWIKI_USER=iamwangbao-163-com`，两条通道立刻对齐。你自己的机器，一行配置。
- **B. 根治（推荐）**：`user_id(repo_path=None)`，在 env 之后、缓存之前插入**仓库本地身份钉** `repowiki/.meta/user_id`（gitignored、可重建，与 `.index.json` 同类）——首次解析成功即写钉，之后任何通道（服务态/登录 shell/hook 子进程）都从同一个 clone 读到同一个 uid。理由：唯一能被两条通道必然共享的东西就是那个 repowiki 目录，继续用环境派生永远会漂。代价：`user_id()` 签名加参数，调用点 6 处要传 repo（task_manager 4 处、note_ingest、capture、telemetry、outcome_report），加一组环境漂移测试。
- **C. 可见性**：`get_task_context`/`add_task_memory` 回显 `user` + `user_source`（env/pin/cache/git/login 哪一档命中的），漂移当场可见而不是沉默成多分片。
- **D. 存量修复**：把 `产品维护` 等任务下已分裂的分片合并到规范 uid（`memories/Administrator.md` → `iamwangbao-163-com.md`，archive 同步），telemetry 别名可先不动。这会改你的真实数据文件，**要你单独批准**我才动。

我的建议顺序：B + C 一起做（一次改动、一次全量测试），A 你自己加一行立刻生效，D 单独一轮。另外压缩那轮的验证还卡在同一个前提：**MCP 进程仍是改前代码**（刚才 `get_task_context` 返回的还是旧 `compaction_work`），需要一次 `/mcp reload`。

The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

<summary>
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - **Request 1 (compaction behavior)**: "现在记忆数量超过阈值会提示用户是否压缩记忆，我觉得可以改成不提示用户确认，直接跑subagent来压缩记忆" — change task-memory compaction so it no longer asks the user for confirmation and instead delegates the work to a subagent. After I reported that the code already claimed "无需用户确认" and that the real cause was the heavy inlined `compaction_work` payload + missing prompt step + "consider compacting" hint wording, the user said **"按推荐方案开发、验证"** — i.e., implement my recommended design (red-only dispatch, payload-free directive, async subagent) and verify it.
   - **Request 2 (identity drift)**: "同一台机器上两条通道的 user_id 不一致 -- 看看如何修改" — diagnose why the same person resolves to two different `user_id` values across the MCP channel and the CLI/in-process channel, and fix it. After I presented four layers (A env止血 / B repo-local pin / C observability / D 存量数据修复), the user said **"按推荐方案 B+C 一起开发"** — implement B (repo-local identity pin, priority after env, before machine cache/git) and C (surface resolved `uid` + `user_source` in responses) together. D (data repair of already-fragmented shards) explicitly requires separate approval and has NOT been approved.
   - Standing session obligation from SessionStart hook: bind session to task 「产品维护」 (done), run async catch-up distillation (done), follow the active-settle protocol (write task memory / draft notes at pause points), all output in Chinese.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - CodeWiki-Plus task-memory layer: `repowiki/tasks/<task_id>/memories/<user_id>.md` per-user shards, file ownership as git-level mutual exclusion, author-exclusive "file-domain" compaction, `memories-archive/<owner>.md` append-only cold layer.
   - Compaction thresholds in `codewiki/mcp/tools/limits.py`: COMPACTION_KEEP=20, threshold count 40, 24KB (24576 B), summary max 4096 chars; graded levels green/yellow/orange/red at 75%/87.5%/100% (ADR-0009 D9).
   - Two-phase stateless compaction (Mode C: prepare → caller writes summary → submit), no LLM inside the tool.
   - ADR-0002 direct-write semantics: reversible operations (archive-backed) bypass the draft→confirm gate; notes keep the gate.
   - MCP lazy-loading meta tools (`mcp_list`/`mcp_get`/`mcp_call`); custom subagents under Qoder cannot get MCP → must use built-in general-purpose subagents.
   - `user_id()` resolution chain and its environment dependence (APPDATA/HOME/PATH/cwd), `%APPDATA%\.codewiki_user_id` machine cache, `git config` needing HOME for `~/.gitconfig`, `os.getlogin()` fallback.
   - `TEAM_LAYOUT_REBUILDABLE_FILES` (config.py:52) → `ensure_gitignore_entries` (team_layout.py:105) → init_wiki.py step 2.5: the canonical mechanism for machine-local, gitignored, rebuildable files.
   - i18n: locales zh/en.yaml hold tool strings (`tools.task_manager.compact_instruction`); prompt bodies remain hard-coded Chinese.
   - Language gate: all output Chinese; explicit `repo_path` on every codewiki MCP call; subagent self-reports must be independently verified before relaying.

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`codewiki/mcp/tools/task_manager.py`** (core change; compaction driver)
     - Driver replaced (was lines 956-971):
       ```python
       # Auto-compaction driver (docs/任务记忆检索与自动压缩设计.md D6): when
       # compaction is due (red), hand the main agent a *delegation* directive — an
       # async worker subagent runs prepare → summary → submit. The directive is
       # payload-free and needs no user confirmation (ADR-0002 extension: the
       # originals all land in memories-archive, so the rewrite is reversible).
       compaction_action: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
       if compaction_due:
           compaction_action = _compaction_action(task_id)
       ```
     - Response key: `**({"compaction_action": compaction_action} if compaction_action else {}),`
     - New builder (placed after `_prepare_compaction_payload`, before `_split_compaction`), including the 4 fixes learned from the live subagent rehearsal:
       ```python
       def _compaction_action(task_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
           """Delegation directive carried by get_task_context when compaction is due.

           Deliberately payload-free: inlining ``entries_to_compress`` into the main
           agent's context costs the very bytes compaction exists to save, and the big
           opaque blob is what nudges agents to ask the user before acting. The worker
           subagent fetches the entries itself via mode="prepare".
           """
           return {
               "task_id": task_id,
               "thresholds": f"{_COMPACTION_THRESHOLD_COUNT} 条 / {_COMPACTION_THRESHOLD_BYTES} B",
               "keep_recent": _COMPACTION_KEEP,
               "summary_max_chars": _COMPACTION_SUMMARY_MAX_CHARS,
               "dispatch": (
                   "立即用 Task 工具发一个**异步**子代理执行压缩：内置 general-purpose 子代理"
                   "（本宿主自定义子代理拿不到 MCP 权限），主 Agent 不等待、不阻塞当前工作。"
                   "给子代理的三步配方："
                   f'① compact_task_memories(mode="prepare", task_id="{task_id}") '
                   "取待压条目——返回值里的 entries_to_compress / instruction / "
                   "summary_max_chars 都以 prepare 的返回为准（不要自己假设数值）；"
                   "若 prepare 返回 compaction_needed=false，**立即停止并汇报原因**，"
                   "不要写摘要、不要 submit（要么阈值未越线，要么待压条目已被并发压缩掉）；"
                   f"② 按 instruction 写「早期记忆（摘要）」，控制在 prepare 返回的 summary_max_chars 内；"
                   f'③ compact_task_memories(mode="submit", task_id="{task_id}", summary=...) 落盘。'
                   "压缩单元是**当前调用者的分片** memories/<user_id>.md（+ legacy memories.md 并入），"
                   "永不动他人文件：prepare 返回 0 条即分片归属与预期不符，按上一条停止汇报。"
               ),
               "gate": ("无需向用户确认，也不得回头征求同意——压缩直写落盘，原文全量进 "
                        "memories-archive/（可回溯，ADR-0002 延伸）。"),
               "report": "子代理回报后向用户一句话同步：压缩条目数与 memories_total 变化。",
           }
       ```
     - `_write_check` soft-limit hint reworded: `"… Write-side counter only — compaction is dispatched by get_task_context at the red level; no action needed here."`
     - `add_task_memory` red hint reworded: `"Hot layer at {hot_total} entries — compaction due: dispatch the async compaction subagent per get_task_context's compaction_action (no user confirmation)."`
     - `_prepare_compaction_payload` docstring now says it is served by `compact_task_memories(mode="prepare")` — the entry point the compaction worker subagent calls.
     - Identity-relevant lines for the B+C work: `_current_user_id()` at :143-147 (imports `user_id()` from `codewiki.src.config`), used at :358, :470, :754, :844, :1088, :1410 — each has `output_dir` in scope and must be changed to pass it.
   - **`codewiki/mcp/registry.py`**

     - `compact_task_memories` description now ends: `Trigger signal: get_task_context returns compaction_due=true plus a compaction_action directive — the caller fulfils it by dispatching an async worker subagent, which calls mode='prepare' itself so the entries to compress never pass through the main agent's context.`
   - **`codewiki/mcp/prompts.py`**

     - New pin machinery + resolver:
       ```python
       _USER_ID_PIN_RELPATH = os.path.join(".meta", "user_id")
       def _user_id_pin_path(output_dir) -> Optional[str]: ...
       def _read_user_id_pin(output_dir) -> str: ...
       def _write_user_id_pin(output_dir, uid: str) -> None: ...
       def resolve_user_id(output_dir=None) -> tuple:
           # priority env | pin | cache | git-email | git-name | login | local
           # writes cache + pin on derivation
       def user_id(output_dir=None) -> str:
           return resolve_user_id(output_dir)[0]
       ```
   - **Docs updated** (口径同步): `README.md` (zh ~525 / en ~1236), `CONTEXT.md` ~108, `docs/任务记忆检索与自动压缩设计.md` (D6 rewritten + dated 修订 block, 验收链 4, 实施切分 row), `docs/capability-matrix.md` row 35 (now `_compaction_action` / `_prepare_compaction_payload`), `docs/任务记忆退役与写入检查设计方案.md` (3 spots).

   - **Knowledge written this session**: draft pitfall note `repowiki/notes/2026-10-10-user-id-解析链整条依赖进程环境ide-服务态-mcp-与登录-shell-会把同一人切成两个记忆分片.md` (status=draft, task_id=产品维护); task memory entries #7kn0 (compaction change) and the earlier probe; distill drafts from the background worker.

user: This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context.
The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - **Request 1 (compaction, completed earlier + retested this session)**: "现在记忆数量超过阈值会提示用户是否压缩记忆，我觉得可以改成不提示用户确认，直接跑subagent来压缩记忆" → then "按推荐方案开发、验证". Final design: red-level only, **payload-free** delegation directive (`compaction_action`) carried by `get_task_context`, dispatched to an **async built-in general-purpose subagent**, no user confirmation (reversible via `memories-archive/`).
   - **Request 2 (identity drift, this session's main work)**: "同一台机器上两条通道的 user_id 不一致 -- 看看如何修改" → then "按推荐方案 B+C 一起开发". B = repo-local identity pin (`repowiki/.meta/user_id`, gitignored/rebuildable, priority right after env); C = surface `user` + `user_source` in tool responses. **D (repair of already-fragmented shards) was explicitly NOT approved.**
   - **Mid-session decision (AskUserQuestion)**: when I found the machine cache `%USERPROFILE%\.codewiki_user_id` was poisoned to `Administrator` and ranked *above* git (so deleting the pin would re-pin the wrong identity), the user chose **"git 优先于缓存（推荐）"** — new chain `env > pin > git(-C repo) > cache > login`, and only git-verified identities may write the pin/cache.
   - **Latest message**: "继续，mcp已重新加载" — i.e. proceed with the two things I said were blocked on reload: (a) land the B+C milestone into task memory, (b) retest the compaction subagent leg.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - CodeWiki-Plus task memory: `repowiki/tasks/<task_id>/memories/<user_id>.md` per-user shards, file ownership = git-level mutual exclusion, author-exclusive "file-domain" compaction, `memories-archive/<owner>.md` append-only cold layer.
   - Compaction thresholds (`codewiki/mcp/tools/limits.py`): COMPACTION_KEEP=20, 40 entries / 24576 B, summary max 4096 chars; graded green/yellow/orange/red (ADR-0009 D9). Two-phase stateless prepare→submit (Mode C), no LLM inside the tool.
   - ADR-0002 direct-write semantics (archive-backed → no confirm gate); notes keep the draft→confirm gate.
   - `user_id` resolution chain and its dependence on process env (APPDATA/HOME/PATH/cwd); `git` needs HOME for `~/.gitconfig`; `os.getlogin()` fallback; `%APPDATA%\.codewiki_user_id` machine cache.
   - `TEAM_LAYOUT_REBUILDABLE_FILES` (config.py:52) → `gitignore_entries()`/`ensure_gitignore_entries()` (team_layout.py) → the canonical mechanism for machine-local, gitignored, rebuildable files.
   - MCP lazy-loading meta tools (`mcp_list`/`mcp_get`/`mcp_call`); Qoder custom subagents lack MCP → must use built-in `general-purpose`.
   - Long-lived MCP server caches imported modules → code changes require `/mcp reload`; hooks run in-process against fresh repo code (this is how the pin got written by a degraded channel).
   - Windows pitfalls: GBK console mangles CJK in Bash output; heredocs in the Bash tool can be swallowed by hook blocks (prefer Write-a-script-then-run); `core.autocrlf=true` with CRLF files.

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`codewiki/src/config.py`** (core of B+C)
     - Added to `TEAM_LAYOUT_REBUILDABLE_FILES`: `".meta/user_id",  # repo-local identity pin (see resolve_user_id); machine-local`
     - `_GIT_USER_NAME_CACHE: dict = {}` / `_GIT_USER_EMAIL_CACHE: dict = {}` keyed by lookup dir (`''` = process cwd).
     - `_git_config_value(key, lookup_dir=None)`: builds `["git","-C",str(lookup_dir),"config",key]` when a dir is given; keeps `CREATE_NO_WINDOW` on Windows.
     - `_git_user_name(lookup_dir=None)` / `_git_user_email(lookup_dir=None)` memoize per dir.
     - Pin machinery: `_USER_ID_PIN_RELPATH = os.path.join(".meta", "user_id")`, `_user_id_pin_path`, `_read_user_id_pin` (sanitizes on read — the uid becomes a filename component, so a foreign pin must not smuggle path separators), `_write_user_id_pin`.
     - Final resolver (approved shape):
       ```python
       def resolve_user_id(output_dir=None) -> tuple:
           env = os.getenv("CODEWIKI_USER", "").strip()
           if env:
               return _sanitize_user_id(env), "env"
           pinned = _read_user_id_pin(output_dir)
           if pinned:
               return pinned, "pin"
           lookup_dir = str(output_dir) if output_dir else None
           email = _git_user_email(lookup_dir)
           if email:
               uid, source = _sanitize_user_id(email), "git-email"
           else:
               name = _git_user_name(lookup_dir)
               if name:
                   uid, source = _sanitize_user_id(name), "git-name"
               else:
                   cached = _read_user_id_cache()
                   if cached:
                       uid, source = cached, "cache"
                   else:
                       login = _login_name()
                       uid, source = (_sanitize_user_id(login), "login") if login else ("local", "local")
           if source in ("git-email", "git-name"):
               _write_user_id_cache(uid)
               _write_user_id_pin(output_dir, uid)
           return uid, source

       def user_id(output_dir=None) -> str:
           return resolve_user_id(output_dir)[0]
       ```
   - **`codewiki/mcp/tools/task_manager.py`**
     - `_current_identity(output_dir=None) -> Tuple[str,str]` wraps `resolve_user_id`; `_current_user_id(output_dir=None)` returns `[0]`.
     - 6 call sites now pass `output_dir`: `_load_memories_layered`, `append` path, `_collect_memory_files` site, `handle_add_task_memory`, `_compact_threshold_state`, search path.
     - `handle_add_task_memory`: `uid, uid_source = _current_identity(output_dir)`; resp gains `"user": uid, "user_source": uid_source` (with a C comment).
     - `handle_get_task_context`: `uid, uid_source = _current_identity(output_dir)` before the return; response gains `"user"`/`"user_source"`; still carries `compaction_action` (payload-free directive built by `_compaction_action(task_id)`).
   - **`codewiki/mcp/tools/telemetry.py:118`** → `uid = user_id(output_dir)`; **`note_ingest.py:452`** → `_author = user_id(output_dir)`; **`capture_conversation.py:428`** → `capture_key = f"{user_id(output_dir)}/{...}"`; **`outcome_report.py:97,101`** → `user_id(output_dir)`.
   - **`codewiki/mcp/registry.py`**: `add_task_memory` description now ends "…Echoes 'user' (the shard written) and 'user_source' (env|pin|git-email|git-name|cache|login|local) — an unexpected source means this channel drifted…"; `get_task_context` description gained the "Also echoes 'user' … with 'user_source' …" sentence (plus the earlier compaction_action/WITHOUT-asking wording).
   - **`codewiki/mcp/prompts.py`**: storage-constraints section gained a bullet documenting the full chain, "只有 git 当场验证过的身份允许写钉/写缓存" rationale, and the `user`+`user_source` echo; earlier this session it also gained the task-workflow step 6 (async compaction subagent, no confirm gate).

- `compaction_action.dispatch` 第②步补一条配方纪律：`existing_summary` 与待压条目是素材不是正文，摘要正文不得含任何 `##`/`###` 标题行；

**刻意没动的两件事**：

user: Base directory for this skill: C:\Users\Administrator\.qoder-cn\skills\open-code-review-delegate

# Open Code Review — Delegation Mode

A skill for performing AI code review where OCR provides deterministic engineering (file filtering, rule resolution) and the host agent performs the actual review using its own intelligence and tools.

## Why Delegation Mode?

If you've used general-purpose agents for code review, you've likely encountered:

- **Incomplete coverage** — On larger changesets, agents tend to "cut corners," selectively reviewing only some files
- **Position drift** — Reported issues frequently don't match the actual code location
- **Unstable quality** — Natural-language-driven skills are hard to debug, and review quality fluctuates with minor prompt variations

Delegation mode solves this by splitting responsibilities:
- **OCR (deterministic code)** handles file selection and rule resolution — tasks that must not go wrong
- **Host agent (LLM)** performs the actual review reasoning — where dynamic decision-making excels

Benefits:
- Zero LLM configuration on OCR side
- Reuses host agent's subscription quota
- Stable, predictable file filtering and rule matching

## Workflow

Follow these seven steps strictly. Do not skip steps or deviate from the protocol.

### Step 1: Preview — Determine What to Review

```bash
ocr delegate preview --format json [--from <ref> --to <ref>] [--commit <hash>] [--exclude <patterns>]
```

This outputs JSON containing:
- **mode** (workspace / range / commit)
- **from / to / commit / merge_base** — ref metadata for constructing git commands
- **reviewable_files** — array of files to review (paths, status, insertions/deletions)
- **excluded_files** — array of files skipped, with exclusion reasons

**Common invocations:**

| Scenario | Command |
|----------|---------|
| Workspace changes (staged, unstaged, untracked) | `ocr delegate preview` |
| Branch comparison | `ocr delegate preview --from main --to feature` |
| Single commit | `ocr delegate preview -c abc123` |

**Important:** Parse the JSON output and extract the `reviewable_files` array. These are the files you MUST review.

### Step 2: Get Rules for Files

```bash
ocr delegate rule --format json <path1> <path2> ...
```

Pass ALL reviewable file paths from Step 1. Output is grouped by rule content — files sharing identical rules appear under one group, avoiding repetition.

Each rule group contains:
- **source** — where the rule comes from (built-in, custom, etc.)
- **pattern** — file pattern this rule matches
- **text** — the actual rule content to apply

**Important:** Build a checklist mapping each file to its rule groups. Use `(path, status)` as the identity — in workspace mode, the same path can appear twice (e.g., staged delete + untracked recreate).

### Step 3: Get Diffs

Choose the diff command based on the mode and refs from Step 1. Use `git --no-pager` for all commands:

- **range mode:** `git --no-pager diff <merge_base>..<to> -- <path>`
- **commit mode:** `git --no-pager show <commit> -- <path>`
- **workspace mode (tracked files):** `git --no-pager diff HEAD -- <path>`
- **workspace mode (untracked files):** Read the entire file content — the whole file is new code

### Step 4: Review Each File Thoroughly

For each file in `reviewable_files`:

1. **Get the diff** using the appropriate command from Step 3
2. **Identify applicable rules** from the rule groups (Step 2)
3. **Review the code** against each rule in the checklist
4. **Mark the file** as `reviewed` or `skipped` (with specific reason)

**Critical constraints:**

- **Review ALL files** — Do not stop after finding the first high-severity issue
- **Process in batches** — Group files by shared rules for efficiency
- **No silent skips** — Every file must have a destination: reviewed or explicitly skipped with justification
- **Deep review** — Read the full file context when needed, not just the diff

### Step 5: Structure Your Findings

For each issue found, output a structured comment with:

**Required fields:**
- `path` — file path where the issue occurs
- `content` — description of the issue and suggested fix

**Optional fields:**
- `start_line` — starting line number
- `end_line` — ending line number
- `category` — one of: bug, security, performance, maintainability, readability, best_practices, other
- `severity` — one of: critical, high, medium, low

**Example:**
```json
{
  "path": "src/auth/login.go",
  "start_line": 45,
  "end_line": 47,
  "category": "security",
  "severity": "high",
  "content": "SQL injection vulnerability: user input is concatenated directly into the query. Use parameterized queries instead."
}
```

### Step 6: Generate Coverage Report

Before presenting results, you MUST verify coverage and output a report:

```
Total files: <total_files>
Reviewed files: <reviewed_files>
Skipped files: <skipped_files>
Coverage rate: <coverage_rate>%
```

**Coverage is mandatory.** You cannot silently skip files. If coverage is below 100%, explain why each file was skipped.

### Step 7 (Optional): Fix Issues

Only when the user explicitly requests "review and fix":

- **Critical/High severity:** Fix directly
- **Medium severity:** Describe the fix, let user decide
- **Low severity:** Skip unless trivial

## Key Principles

1. **Deterministic engineering first** — Trust OCR's file selection and rule resolution. Do not second-guess which files to review.

2. **Complete coverage** — Every reviewable file must be processed. No exceptions.

3. **Rule-driven review** — Apply the rules from Step 2 systematically. Do not invent your own review criteria beyond what OCR provides.

4. **Precise locations** — When reporting issues, provide accurate line numbers. Verify against the actual code.

5. **No early termination** — Do not stop after finding the first issue. Complete the full review.

## Troubleshooting

**OCR CLI not found:**
Install via `npm install -g @alibaba-group/open-code-review` or download from GitHub releases.

**Empty reviewable_files list:**
Check that you have uncommitted changes or specify the correct refs with `--from` and `--to`.

**Rules seem incomplete:**
OCR's rule engine is deterministic. If rules seem missing, check `.opencodereview/rule.json` for custom rules.

## Resources

- GitHub: https://github.com/alibaba/open-code-review
- Documentation: https://open-codereview.ai/docs
- Delegation mode docs: https://open-codereview.ai/docs/delegate

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Task memory storage: `repowiki/tasks/<task_id>/memories/<user_id>.md` (file-domain, author-exclusive shards), `memories-archive/<owner>.md` (append-only cold layer), legacy `memories.md` convergence, `_stage_removal` index companion.
   - Compaction thresholds in `codewiki/mcp/tools/limits.py` (re-exported privately by task_manager): COUNT=40, BYTES=24576, KEEP=20, SUMMARY_MAX_CHARS=4096; `needed = total_entries > 20 and (count>=40 or bytes>=24576)`; graded yellow/orange/red (ADR-0009 D9); two-phase stateless prepare→submit (Mode C).
   - `_split_compaction` (ADR-0009 D8): superseded entries first, then oldest chronological, take `len - KEEP`.
   - `entry_sort_key` (store.py:327): dated → `(0, ts)`, **undated → `(1, "")` = always last** ⇒ undated entries can never reach the compress set (sticky dead weight).
   - `split_entries` / `split_summary_and_entries` (store.py:286–320): summary = `SUMMARY_HEADING` up to the first `### `; `###` inside summary text leaks into the entry list.
   - Identity resolution: `resolve_user_id(output_dir) -> (uid, source)`; `_USER_ID_CACHE_PATH` frozen at **import time** from `APPDATA or expanduser("~")` → the physical reason one machine held two cache files/two identities; pin read-sanitized (uid becomes a filename component).
   - `TEAM_LAYOUT_REBUILDABLE_FILES` → gitignored/rebuildable registration; `locked_rmw`/`locked_write` sidecar locks; auto_push vs auto_stage mutual exclusion (schema.yaml `conventions.git_sync.auto_push=true`).
   - Long-lived MCP server caches imported modules → `/mcp reload` required; Qoder custom subagents lack MCP → built-in general-purpose only.
   - OCR delegation protocol: `ocr delegate preview [--exclude]`, `ocr delegate rule`, workspace diffs via `git --no-pager diff HEAD -- <path>`, untracked = read whole file, mandatory coverage report, no silent skips, precision-over-recall rule.
   - Windows/host pitfalls: GBK console mangles CJK (write UTF-8 result files and Read them); tests must run `.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pytest` (full suite ≈ 11 min); `uv run pytest` trampoline fails.

       new_summary = str(arguments.get("summary") or "").strip()

user: This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context.
The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - （承接上文）`open-code-review-delegate` skill + 「审查代码」：对工作区变更做**确定性范围、规则驱动、全覆盖**的 OCR 委托模式评审，只审查、不修（Step 7 需用户明示「review and fix」）。
   - 「代码可以提交吗」：要我给出可提交性判定（验证证据），并在提交前对齐范围。随后经 AskUserQuestion 明确两个决策：**「先修 Medium 再提交（推荐）」**、**「按主题拆 3 个提交（推荐）」**，且 **`.scratch` 一律不入库**。
   - 「.scratch忽略提交 ， uv.lock 提交」：给 `.scratch` 加 gitignore 规则并提交；`uv.lock` 单独提交。
   - 「提交」：把剩下 3 个 repowiki 待提交项（draft 笔记、任务分片增量、wiki log）提交。
   - 长期有效约束（须继续遵守）：所有产出用**中文**；每次 codewiki MCP 调用**必须显式传 `repo_path`**；**未经明示不得 commit/push**；**禁止手写任务记忆文件**（只能走 add_task_memory 等工具）；**子代理自报必须磁盘独立核验**；`repowiki/.meta/telemetry/` 的 6 个历史身份分片与 15 条更旧草稿笔记是**用户明示未批准项，勿擅自合并/确认**；代码改动需用户 `/mcp reload` 才在 MCP 通道生效；测试用 `.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pytest`（全量约 12 分钟），`uv run pytest` 在本机 trampoline 失败。

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - OCR 委托模式七步协议：`ocr delegate preview [--exclude]` → `ocr delegate rule` → `git --no-pager diff HEAD -- <path>`（workspace 模式；未跟踪文件读全文）→ 逐文件按规则组评审 → 结构化 findings → 强制覆盖率报告 → 仅明示才修。
   - 免确认压缩新链路：`get_task_context` 仅在 **red** 档携带 **payload-free 的 `compaction_action`** 派发指令；主 Agent 异步 spawn 内置 general-purpose 子代理跑 `prepare → 写摘要 → submit`；可逆性来自原文全量进 `memories-archive/`（ADR-0002 延伸）。
   - 压缩阈值（`codewiki/mcp/tools/limits.py`）：COUNT=40、BYTES=24*1024、KEEP=20、SUMMARY_MAX_CHARS=4096；`_compaction_needed = total>KEEP and (total>COUNT or bytes>BYTES)`；`_compaction_level` 分 yellow/orange/red。
   - 身份解析链 `config.resolve_user_id(output_dir)`：`env > 仓库本地钉 repowiki/.meta/user_id（gitignored、可重建）> git -C <output_dir> user.email > user.name > 机器缓存 > os.getlogin > local`；**只有 git-email/git-name 两种当场验证过的结果允许写钉/写缓存**；`user` + `user_source` 回显（C：身份可见性）。
